# Tier-1 programme ledger

Updated 2026-09-23 · 377 items · generated by `scripts/check_tier1_program.py --render` (do not edit by hand; edit the JSON).

Status vocabulary: EXISTS-LIT = code runs on a named clock and its artifact has a consumer; EXISTS-DARK = code exists, no clock or no consumer; PARTIAL = part exists (gap named); MISSING = nothing; LANDED = built in this programme, with commit.

> **What LANDED does not mean.** LANDED means the code exists in THIS REPOSITORY, on a named clock, with an artifact and a consumer. It does NOT mean the artifact has ever been produced on the trading box, and it does not mean the behaviour has been observed with money at risk. That distinction is what the five acceptance_properties measure, and they are measured from artifacts rather than asserted: an absent artifact reads UNMEASURED, never MET. Read the census as build coverage and the acceptance properties as operational proof. They are different questions and the second is the one that decides whether the desk works.

## Acceptance properties

*These are the operational half. They are measured from artifacts by `scripts/check_acceptance_properties.py`, hourly; an absent artifact reads UNMEASURED and never MET.*

- **AP1 No fixed discovery frontier** — MET. Measure: A new asset, mechanism, feature, dataset, language, model class, execution idea or research method reaches the docket without a code change: count of frontier cells opened per week by the system itself (hunt_frontier vectors, universe registry additions, data_universe_map entries, new families registered) vs by a human session.
  - 2026-09-09T01:59:16+00:00 MEASURED: {"cells_opened_7d": 248, "frontier_vectors": 0, "frontier_vectors_new_7d": 0, "frontier_vectors_hunted": 0, "universe_symbols": 251, "universe_symbols_new_7d": 248, "data_universe_map_entries": null, "registered_families": 34} -- frontier vectors are named by the hunters themselves and universe rows by the bar refresh -- both open without a code change; families still need one
- **AP2 No unmeasured intelligence** — PARTIAL. Measure: Every LLM seat, miner, dataset, generator and infrastructure change has a row with incremental survivors and incremental robust E[log W] attributed to it (compiled artifact per_source/seats, hunt_frontier yield, compute_ledger cost); anything without a row is EXISTS-DARK by definition.
  - 2026-09-09T01:59:16+00:00 MEASURED: {"sources_with_yield_row": 86, "seats": [], "seats_dark": [], "modules": 71, "modules_measured": 1, "modules_unmeasured": ["ai_capital_modifier", "alpha_breadth", "alpha_evolution", "alt_data_hypothesis", "authority_ramp", "combine_survivors", "conditional_state_edge", "cost_stress", "cross_asset_signal", "data_source:broker_clock", "data_source:data_prospector", "data_source:event_calendar", "dat -- MET needs every seat donating, every module with a rent verdict and every leg with a cost row; the lists name what is dark
- **AP3 No semantic breaks** — MISSING. Measure: A hypothesis id can be followed deterministically source -> compiler row -> gauntlet cell -> certificate -> forward clock -> allocation fraction -> intent -> broker order -> position -> deal -> realized R -> lesson/graph update; measured as the share of closed strategy deals with a complete chain (target 100%).
  - 2026-09-09T01:59:16+00:00 UNMEASURED: {} -- reports/attribution_chain.json absent -- the daily cycle has not run the attribution join on this host
- **AP4 The portfolio creates research missions** — MISSING. Measure: The allocator's unfilled heat and the book's missing exposures (session, regime, factor beta, horizon, drawdown states) are written as missions that generators consume; measured as missions issued per week and candidates tagged with the mission that caused them.
  - 2026-09-09T01:59:16+00:00 UNMEASURED: {"candidates_tagged": 0} -- no missions artifact at any of ('reports/RESEARCH_MISSIONS.json', 'reports/research_missions.json', 'data/research_missions.json', 'reports/missions.json', 'data/missions.json')
- **AP5 The machine improves the machine** — PARTIAL. Measure: Generators, validators, schedulers and allocation methods run as A/B arms with equal compute and the inferior arm is retired by survivor yield / realized Elog; measured as the number of arms with a recorded verdict.
  - 2026-09-09T01:59:16+00:00 MEASURED: {"arms": 12, "arms_with_verdict": 0, "rent_retire_named": 0} -- arms judged by survivor yield / realised Elog, plus organs the rent ledger names

- **NE1 NET IS THE CURRENCY, ONE FUNCTION: net = gross - spread/slippage at the cell's own size and state - market impact at that size - financing and swap over the holding period - commission - the multiplicity charge already owed. `libs/research/net_edge.py` returns the net figure, the full decomposition and an UNMEASURED verdict PER TERM the data cannot support. An unpriced term is never a zero: it makes the net a BOUND, and because four of the five terms can only reduce the edge while financing alone may be a credit, a negative bound with financing unpriced is COST_DEAD_UNCONFIRMED and not COST_DEAD. MEASURED 2026-09-23 on the real tree: 273 rows priced, 23 sign flips after costs (14 COST_DEAD, 9 unconfirmed), 55 net-positive.** — LANDED

The worker pool proper is I3 and is gated on hardware this desk lacks.

- **BA1 THE 24/7 MAXIMISER (principal 2026-09-23). Every hour it measures the desk's four standing bottlenecks -- conversion debt, judging throughput in hours-of-queue at the measured rate, forward enrolment latency, and plumbing defects -- names the single BINDING one on a common saturating severity, and moves compute toward it through the budget machinery that already exists: `research_auction.build` blends this organ's compute_shift with bottleneck_law's by MAXIMUM, and the auction's factors are what `research_budget.budget_s` multiplies. Plumbing past its escalation window scores 1.0 and binds outright, because every other measurement is then a measurement of a stopped machine. Every shift is >= 1.0: the organ has no instrument for starving a department (growth governance Rule 2). The trend of all four over the last day is published, so 'is it getting better' is answerable every morning. MEASURED first pass 2026-09-23: BINDING = plumbing_defects at severity 0.78, meta raised to x1.783.** — LANDED
  - desks/mt5/research/bottleneck_attack.py:86
  - desks/mt5/research/bottleneck_attack.py:296
  - desks/mt5/research/bottleneck_attack.py:334
  - desks/mt5/research/research_auction.py:196
  - clock: hourly_cycle:bottleneck_attack · artifact: desks/mt5/reports/BOTTLENECK_ATTACK.json · consumer: desks/mt5/research/research_auction.py (compute_shift blended into the bids) -> desks/mt5/research/research_budget.py budget_s; the canonical registry's research_memory under memory_key bottleneck_attack:binding

- **AR1 THE LAWFUL-GROUND PRE-FILTER IS DELETED; CLASSIFICATION NOW ROUTES USE AND NEVER STOPS MINING (principal's order 2026-09-23). LAWS 5e carried six brakes the desk had imposed on ITSELF, none of which was a legal requirement: an ACCESS_UNCLEAR QUARANTINE that kept a row's metadata and threw its claim away (allowed_uses = one entry, register, at evidence_weight_cap 0.0); a machine-extraction veto that stripped machine_extract and store_raw from every PUBLIC_WITH_TERMS row; a robots.txt Disallow read as that same refusal; machine_use_allowed=false read as 'registered, never scraped'; an eight-domain clone allowlist in the research sandbox; and a licence gate that refused to RUN public research code, which the module's own text called 'the single reason every seed is UNDISPOSED today'. Things published on the open internet are lawful to read. Licence, robots, source class and credibility are now ROUTING AND PROVENANCE LABELS -- they say what may be REDISTRIBUTED (redistribute_allowed, terms_note) and how much WEIGHT the evidence carries -- and they never stop discovery, ingestion, representation or testing. Every label but the refused three grants the full MINING_USES vocabulary at a non-zero cap; quarantine is False on every routing row and the ledger it fed is empty by construction. What remains is about ACTS, not content, and is exactly five (HARD_BOUNDARY, pinned by HARD_BOUNDARY_COUNT): no credential theft or logging in as someone else; no bypassing an access control or a paywall; no material non-public information; no stolen or leaked private data; no personal data harvesting or doxxing. A page behind a login or paywall is never broken into and its open surface is mined like any other. The six deleted brakes are named as DATA in REMOVED_BRAKES and in the law text, so a later session cannot re-derive the old caution from silence. ONE FIX WENT THE OTHER WAY, and it is the one that had to: the hard-boundary marker scan never read `claim`, the router's OWN primary content key, so a credential dump or an MNPI line arriving in the exact shape route() builds reached none of the marker scans and was labelled on its url alone. Widening the boundary's reach is the single direction that module may still move.** — LANDED

- **AR2 NOTHING IS QUEUED: THE QUEUE CENSUS AND ITS FENCE (principal's order 2026-09-23, 'keep no queues ... nothing should be queued in the research system, all immediate tested'). A row is processed ON ARRIVAL; where a budget genuinely runs out the leftover is the FIRST work of the next pass and its AGE IS PUBLISHED, never an indefinite park. The desk had no idea how much of its own work was parked, because each organ knew its own number, in its own unit, and no artifact put them side by side. queue_census.py measures every queue in the desk -- depth, the age of the OLDEST ROW, and a drain rate DIFFERENCED against the previous census rather than declared -- and check_no_queues.py fails when any queue's oldest row is older than ONE CYCLE of the organ that owns it, when a queue has open rows and NO DRAINER at all (PARKED), or when rows carry no enqueue timestamp so their age cannot be measured (UNMEASURED, which fails: L1.28a). It CONSUMES the artifacts other builders own -- CONVERSION_MAXIMISER.json, GAUNTLET_BACKPRESSURE.json, COVERAGE_DRAIN.json, FORWARD_ENROLMENT.json, QUEUE.json -- and never opens their stores or second-guesses their numbers. THE FIRST PASS SHIPS RED AND THE RED IS THE WORK: 14 queues, 63,110 rows waiting, 6 BREACH, 1 PARKED, 2 UNMEASURED, oldest row 654 h (27 days). research_queue.json holds 21,373 open rows with NO SCHEDULED DRAINER -- its only readers are report builders and an archive restorer that puts rows BACK. registry.claim_candidates(), the lease meant to drain 5,769 queued sqlite candidates, has zero callers outside registry.py. miner_deepening_queue.json holds 28,450 rows carrying no per-row timestamp, so their age was literally unknowable. A fence tuned to pass on today's backlog would pin that backlog in place (L1.43), so it is not tuned.** — LANDED
  - desks/mt5/research/queue_census.py:99 - QUEUES, every queue in the desk as data with its writer, its drainer (None where there is none) and its owner's cycle
  - desks/mt5/research/queue_census.py:243 - _byte_budget derived from measured free physical memory, floored, never from a machine size
  - desks/mt5/research/queue_census.py:430 - drain_rate differenced against the previous census; None is UNMEASURED, never a comfortable zero
  - desks/mt5/research/queue_census.py:455 - verdict(): BREACH / PARKED / UNMEASURED

  - landed: the queue census and its fence wired 2026-09-23
- **CT7 ONE CANON, RESTORED BY THE ORGAN ITSELF (principal 2026-09-23: 'everything remains empty -- open, close, all automatic, don't just report'; LAWS 7 A REPORT IS NOT A REMEDY). The certificate lane had TWO pens: the sealed gauntlet, and its unfixed fork desks/mt5/side_channels/ug_remote.py, wired as the hourly leg auto_ug_remote, which rewrote reports/UNIVERSAL_SURVIVORS.json from an empty dict with no gate_policy key. The canon went 56 -> 0 on 2026-09-17 and was still being re-emptied hourly six days later while 13 sleeves traded and 855 forward clocks ran. Losing the ATTESTATION was the worse half: every reader fell through to the seal, and the sealed gauntlet's own restore loop reads retired_certificates out of the file the wipe had just emptied, so it could restore nothing for ever. The fork now carries the same three guards its sealed twin got in 2026-08-25 (retain, attest, refuse empty, refuse shrink), and certificate_truth.repair() closes the damage on its OWN hourly clock with no --apply and no hand, through the lane's own rules only: the authority ratchet's seal restore (whose only clock is a VPS systemd timer, on the wrong machine), a git harvest for what a bad writer overwrote in place, and the sealed gauntlet's own restore predicate re-asking each retirement its own question. It can only ADD evidence the desk already earned: it never retires a row, never lowers a count, never touches sleeves.json or the gateway. And an EMPTY canon is now UNMEASURED, never 'nothing is certified' -- apply() withholds every retirement unless the lane is SETTLED, because the migration of 2026-09-23 08:37 retired 597 forward clocks and 73 ledger claims against an authority file a second writer had emptied.** — LANDED

No store certifies by declaring -- the scalp lane's self-declaration included: every claim the canon does not hold is SUBMITTED to the one judge (the sealed gauntlet) on the hourly clock, and a claim older than one judging cycle that reached no queue is the fatal CLAIM_NOT_SUBMITTED_TO_JUDGE. A ban is a decision, never re-judged; an unstamped claim is UNMEASURED, never a stale verdict.** — LANDED

### Phase RD — The RD-Agent closure programme (principal 2026-09-22): make every research civilization behave like one experiment directly changing the next -- one canonical ExperimentSpec, world-miner conversion to the campaign queue, the experiment memory graph, credit assignment end to end, priors updated by every completed experiment, and the funnel metric that settles it

- **RD1 THE CANONICAL EXPERIMENT OBJECT: one `ExperimentSpec` every factor, model, world-miner lead, physics law, macro idea, country mechanism and sandbox hypothesis compiles into -- lineage (parents/children/method/source), data snapshot identity (PIT vintage + content hash), model, features/representation, target, state and regime conditioning, costs, falsifier, trial family, novelty axes and status. `from_discovery` / `from_candidate` / `from_claim` are the constructors and `to_campaign()` produces the registry candidate (registry.enqueue_candidate) with full provenance. NO PROSE-ONLY RESEARCH: a row that cannot compile returns a `CompileDefect` naming the reason (PROSE_ONLY, NO_INSTRUMENT, NO_FAMILY, NO_FALSIFIER, NO_DATA, OFF_UNIVERSE, UNREADABLE) and the missing fields, which the sweep records as conversion debt rather than dropping** — LANDED

- **RD13 ACTIVE EXPERIMENT DESIGN: when two hypotheses compete the desk runs the experiment that maximises expected information gain about WHICH is true, not both blindly. Three recoveries were wrong when first measured and the physics, not the test, was fixed: a 1-D sinusoid IS time-reversible (the estimator must not read periodicity as an arrow of time -- a ratchet separates by 470x, the sinusoid does not separate at all), and both LinearResponse and InverseProblems were selecting their driver by a CONTEMPORANEOUS correlation over RAW columns, which is the spurious-regression trap: a near-unit-root price level beat the column carrying the planted impulse, which ranked last of thirteen. Drivers are now stationary fluctuations (`_fluct`, differencing anything with |acf(1)| > 0.98) and are chosen by the scientist's own question -- the strongest LAGGED correlation for linear response, the same validation slice that picks the ridge for deconvolution. WIRED IN BOTH LEGS AND MEASURED: physics_lab --budget-s 600 runs 601 s (was 846 s at a 420 s budget before the judge and the reviewers were put on the same wall clock) inside its 700 s cap, and math_lab with the physics wing runs 543 s at --budget-s 600 inside the 720 s default timeout -- the leg asked for 3,000 s, which `_producer_impl` could only ever kill before MATH_LAB.json was written. TWO CAUSES, both real. (1) scripts/recommendations.py's read-modify-write lock did `import fcntl`, which is POSIX-only: every MUTATING subcommand (add / dispose / correct / repoint / claim) died with ModuleNotFoundError on Windows while `report` and `verify`, which skip the lock, worked perfectly, so the ledger looked healthy from the box and was physically unwritable there. `_flock_exclusive` now takes the same exclusive, non-blocking, closed-on-exit lock through msvcrt where fcntl is absent. (2) The only drain on any clock was ops/run_recommendation_worker.sh, a VPS cron that hands the open rows to an LLM; the VPS has published no commit since 2026-09-08, so the queue's only consumer had stopped and nothing said so.** — LANDED

- **U82 THE TEN SOURCE LAYERS ARE MEASURED FROM THE OUTSIDE, NOT DECLARED BY THE PACK. Every pack writes `verified: False` into its own source rows and always will -- a pack has no way of knowing whether anything ever fetched the root it named -- so `coverage.covered()` read DECLARED_UNVERIFIED for almost every layer on the desk and the ten-layer depth claim was an assertion rather than a measurement. `coverage_drain.verify_layers` decides it from the registry instead: a layer is MAPPED when a row whose host matches one of that layer's declared roots carries a real `last_crawled` stamp, ABSENT_DECLARED when the pack named the layer absent with a reason, REFUSED_HARD_BOUNDARY when every declared host sits behind one of the five refused ACTS, DECLARED_UNVERIFIED when roots exist and none has been fetched, and UNMAPPED when the pack said nothing at all. Five states and no quiet zero. MEASURED across 59 packs: 168 MAPPED, 2 ABSENT_DECLARED, 420 DECLARED_UNVERIFIED, 0 UNMAPPED -- and the 420 is the artifact's own named largest gap, with the worst packs listed, which is what makes it work rather than a number. A REFUSAL IS EXCLUDED FROM MAPPED: the first live pass stamped 113 pack sources as refused in the same step it registered them, which moved layers_mapped 57 -> 184 without a page having been read; `crawled_hosts` now excludes refusals and `reopen_deleted_brakes` returned all 113 to the queue, because the brake that refused them was deleted by LAWS 5e the same day and leaving them stamped would have made the repeal cosmetic. EXTENDED THE SAME DAY to THREE parity axes on one ratchet (principal: "depth AND breadth both maximum for all and equal ... and all datasets possible ingested too"). DEPTH scores seven components measured from outside the pack; BREADTH scores mechanisms, instruments, sectors, source classes, languages and interaction partners against an EXPLICIT maximum so every pack's distance from the complete pack is publishable; INGESTION enumerates the ten publisher classes every jurisdiction has and marks each INGESTED (a declared host carries a crawl stamp), ENUMERATED_NOT_REACHED with what is missing, NO_LAWFUL_GROUND, or MISSING. Each axis carries its own floor set at the WEAKEST department's score, and each floor rises the moment that minimum rises and never falls -- so raising the worst pack permanently raises the standard for all of them. Seeded 2026-09-23 over 70 packs: depth 0.43/0.768/0.97, breadth 0.339/0.814/1.00, ingestion 0.21/0.70/1.00 (min/median/max); worst by summed distance no, se, za, tw, kr.** — LANDED
  - desks/mt5/research/coverage_drain.py:892 - verify_layers(): the five states, measured
  - desks/mt5/research/coverage_drain.py:660 - reopen_deleted_brakes(): a repealed brake leaves its damage in the data
  - desks/mt5/research/coverage_drain.py:971 - largest_gap(): expected value across three gap families
  - libs/research/coverage.py:686 - covered(): the two-condition rule this feeds

Every one is two-sided -- the stronger forward posterior, the more orthogonal exposure and the more independent return stream are tilted UP by exactly what their peers are tilted down. Nothing here caps, shrinks or vetoes, and the 20% heat floor, the gold lot floor and the daily-loss parameters are untouched.
